Multimodal Prompt Engineer with practical experience working with generative AI across text, image and video production. My work focuses on turning creative ideas into controlled, long-form visual content through structured prompting, repeated refinement and close attention to character identity, facial appearance, clothing, environments, locations, backgrounds and scene continuity.</p></div><div class="mt-6 space-y-4"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><p class="leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">My professional path combines generative AI content creation, AI video production, content writing, research and digital content quality review. My background in content writing, research and digital content review also strengthens how I approach AI-generated work — I read requirements carefully, separate evidence from assumption, identify weak reasoning, and communicate findings in a structured and practical way.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-6"><div class="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-compass h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m16.24 7.76-1.804 5.411a2 2 0 0 1-1.265 1.265L7.76 16.24l1.804-5.411a2 2 0 0 1 1.265-1.265z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle></svg>How I Work</div><div class="space-y-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><p>My work begins with planning. Before generating any scene, I plan the story, characters, settings, visual cues and overall creative direction. I keep useful references so that each generated scene can be checked against the intended look, tone and continuity rather than accepted on first impression.</p><p>From there, I work in stages — text-to-image, image-to-video, voice production, sequencing and review. I review outputs critically, identify inconsistencies in character identity, facial appearance, clothing, environments or backgrounds, and correct them before moving forward. This reduces unnecessary retries and helps maintain stronger continuity across long-form content.</p></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-fuchsia-400/20 bg-fuchsia-500/5 p-6"><div class="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-target h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="6"></circle><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"></circle></svg>Professional Direction</div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">My long-term direction is to keep building strong, character-consistent, long-form AI content — Nollywood-style films, story-driven video and multimodal productions — while refining my prompting techniques across text, image and video. text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-shield-alert h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1z"></path><path d="M12 8v4"></path><path d="M12 16h.01"></path></svg><span class="hidden sm:inline">Voice &amp; Sequencing</span><span class="sm:hidden">Voice</span></button></div></div><div class="mt-8"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(12px)"><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2"><div class="space-y-6"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-6"><h4 class="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">What multimodal prompt engineering means to me</h4><p class="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Multimodal prompting is not writing a single instruction and accepting the first result. It is the disciplined process of defining the creative objective, preparing references and constraints, generating the first output with the appropriate tool, reviewing the result critically, and refining prompts, assets or direction until the output meets the intended standard. A polished output can still miss the brief, break character identity, drift in environment or fail continuity — my role is to catch and correct those issues before they become final content.</p></div><div class="rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="mb-4 font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">My multimodal workflow</h4><ol class="relative space-y-4 border-l border-violet-400/20 pl-6"><li class="relative"><span class="absolute -left-[1.65rem] grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">1</span><p class="font-medium text-foreground">Clarify the creative objective</p><p class="mt-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Identify the story, tone, characters, setting, visual cues and the intended emotional effect before any generation begins.</p></li><li class="relative"><span class="absolute -left-[1.65rem] grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">2</span><p class="font-medium text-foreground">Prepare references and constraints</p><p class="mt-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Collect useful references for character identity, clothing, environments, locations and backgrounds so each output can be checked against intent.</p></li><li class="relative"><span class="absolute -left-[1.65rem] grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">3</span><p class="font-medium text-foreground">Generate the first output</p><p class="mt-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Use the appropriate generative stage — text-to-image, image-to-video, voice production — with a prompt aligned to the objective.</p></li><li class="relative"><span class="absolute -left-[1.65rem] grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">4</span><p class="font-medium text-foreground">Review critically</p><p class="mt-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Check coherence, realism, character recognisability, facial appearance, clothing, environment continuity, narrative alignment and audience suitability.</p></li><li class="relative"><span class="absolute -left-[1.65rem] grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">5</span><p class="font-medium text-foreground">Refine and correct</p><p class="mt-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Adjust prompts, references or direction to address weaknesses, inconsistencies or failures identified during review.</p></li><li class="relative"><span class="absolute -left-[1.65rem] grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">6</span><p class="font-medium text-foreground">Finalise only when ready</p><p class="mt-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Accept the output only after it meets the intended quality standard and supports the long-form story direction.</p></li></ol></div></div><div class="space-y-6"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-6"><h4 class="mb-4 font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">What I look for in generated outputs</h4><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2"><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Character recognisability</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Does the generated character remain identifiable across scenes through facial characteristics, clothing and visual cues?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Facial appearance</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Is the face consistent with the intended identity, without unintended drift between scenes?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Clothing and styling</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Does the wardrobe, accessories and styling remain coherent with the story and the character?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Environment continuity</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Are locations, backgrounds, settings, objects and lighting consistent with the established scene?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Narrative alignment</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Does the output serve the story, tone and intended emotional effect?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Realism and artifacts</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Is the output free of obvious AI artifacts, distortion, morphing or implausible motion?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Audience suitability</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Is the content appropriate for the intended audience and platform?</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>Production fit</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Does the output integrate cleanly into the longer sequence, voice track and final edit?</p></div></div></div><div class="rounded-2xl border border-fuchsia-400/20 bg-fuchsia-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Professional value</h4><p class="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Good multimodal prompting improves the reliability of long-form AI content. It helps reduce unnecessary retries, keeps character identity stable, holds environments and backgrounds together, and produces scenes that fit the story. My value is in bringing careful planning, critical review and structured refinement to that process — not just generating outputs, but explaining why an output works, where it fails, and what needs to change.</p></div><div class="rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/20 bg-sky-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Related experience</h4><p class="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">My multimodal prompting experience includes building and managing AI-powered YouTube channels with more than 29,000 subscribers and more than 3.5 million combined views, and creating more than 20 Nollywood-style AI films using story development, scripting, prompting, image generation, video generation, voice production, sequencing and review. I work across text, image and video generation in one production process, selecting outputs from different generative stages according to the production requirement.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="prompt-refinement" class="relative scroll-mt-24 py-20 sm:py-28 border-t border-border/40"><div class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8"><div class="max-w-3xl"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.18em] bg-fuchsia-500/15 text-fuchsia-200 border-fuchsia-400/30"><span class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-pulse-glow"></span>Prompt Refinement &amp; Generative AI</div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><h2 class="font-display text-3xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight text-foreground sm:text-4xl md:text-[2.7rem]">Turning observed results into<!-- --> <span class="text-gradient">clearer, refined instructions</span></h2></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><p class="mt-5 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground sm:text-lg">Prompt refinement is the process of improving prompts based on what a generative tool actually produces. My approach focuses on observing outputs critically, tracing weaknesses back to the prompt, and revising instructions to address the specific issue rather than changing everything blindly.</p></div></div><div class="mt-12 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-fuchsia-400/20 bg-fuchsia-500/5 p-7"><div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-400/30 bg-fuchsia-500/15 text-fuchsia-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-square-terminal h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7 11 2-2-2-2"></path><path d="M11 13h4"></path><rect width="18" height="18" x="3" y="3" rx="2" ry="2"></rect></svg></div><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">How I understand prompt refinement</h3></div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">A prompt is not a one-shot instruction — it is a working document that evolves with the production. A weak prompt may leave too much room for interpretation, causing the model to drift in character identity, environment, tone or continuity. A refined prompt defines the objective, anchors the references, sets boundaries, and explains what a successful output should look like — and it improves with each generation cycle.</p><h4 class="mt-6 mb-4 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">Prompt design principles</h4><div class="space-y-2.5"><div class="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="text-sm font-medium text-fuchsia-200">Define the objective</p><p class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">The prompt should state the exact creative goal — story beat, character action, environment, tone and intended emotional effect.</p></div><div class="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="text-sm font-medium text-fuchsia-200">Anchor references</p><p class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Useful references keep the output aligned to the intended character identity, clothing, environment and visual cues.</p></div><div class="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="text-sm font-medium text-fuchsia-200">Set constraints clearly</p><p class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">The prompt should explain what must be included and what should be avoided — drift, distortion, unwanted style, off-brief additions.</p></div><div class="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="text-sm font-medium text-fuchsia-200">Specify output structure</p><p class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Where the output is structured — shot framing, character placement, scene composition — the prompt should define it explicitly.</p></div><div class="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="text-sm font-medium text-fuchsia-200">Add success criteria</p><p class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">The prompt should make it possible to judge whether the output succeeded against the brief.</p></div><div class="rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="text-sm font-medium text-fuchsia-200">Iterate from evidence</p><p class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Prompt refinement should respond to actual output behavior — observed drift, artifact, identity loss or continuity break — not guesswork.</p></div></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-7"><div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-violet-400/30 bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></div><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Prompt debugging workflow</h3></div><ol class="space-y-3"><li class="flex gap-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">1</span>Review the output to identify what failed: character recognisability, facial appearance, clothing, environment, motion, narrative alignment, artifact risk.</li><li class="flex gap-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">2</span>Trace the failure back to the prompt: missing context, unclear instruction, weak references, poor output guidance.</li><li class="flex gap-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">3</span>Rewrite the prompt to correct the specific weakness rather than changing everything blindly.</li><li class="flex gap-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">4</span>Run another output and compare whether the revised prompt improved the result.</li><li class="flex gap-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-violet-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold text-violet-200">5</span>Continue refinement until the prompt reliably produces the intended quality standard.</li></ol><h4 class="mt-6 mb-3 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">Use cases</h4><ul class="space-y-2"><li class="flex gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" 4-4"></path></svg>Prompt stress testing and model stumping to surface weaknesses under demanding instructions.</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-card/40 style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><h3 class="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">Practical Generative AI Workflows for Long-Form Content &amp; AI Video Production</h3></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><p class="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground sm:text-base">My generative AI work includes AI-powered YouTube channels with more than 29,000 subscribers and more than 3.5 million combined views, more than 20 Nollywood-style AI films, long-form character-driven storytelling, image generation, video generation, voice production, sequencing and review. I work across text, image and video generation in one production process, selecting outputs from different generative stages according to the production requirement.</p></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><p class="mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">The important part of my workflow is not simply generating content. It is the review process that comes after generation. I check whether the output is coherent, visually consistent, narratively useful and appropriate for the audience. When an output fails, I identify whether the weakness came from the prompt, the tool limitation, the reference material, or the editing process — and I correct it before moving forward.</p></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-6"><p class="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Tools I use</p><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><span class="rounded-full border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-sky-200">Text-to-Image</span><span class="rounded-full border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-sky-200">Image-to-Video</span><span class="rounded-full border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-sky-200">Voice Production</span><span class="rounded-full border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-sky-200">Sequencing</span><span class="rounded-full border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-sky-200">Critical Review</span></div></div></div></div><div class="lg:col-span-5"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-background/50 p-5"><p class="font-display text-3xl font-bold text-gradient">29,000+</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Subscribers across AI YouTube channels</p></div><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-background/50 p-5"><p class="font-display text-3xl font-bold text-gradient">3.5M+</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Combined views</p></div><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-background/50 p-5"><p class="font-display text-3xl font-bold text-gradient">20+</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Nollywood-style AI films</p></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-3 rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-5"><p class="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-violet-200">Workflow structure</p><ol class="space-y-1.5"><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">1<!-- -->.</span>Define the creative objective — story, characters, setting, tone, visual cues.</li><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">2<!-- -->.</span>Identify the target audience and intended output format.</li><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">3<!-- -->.</span>Prepare the prompt, references, constraints and success criteria.</li><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">4<!-- -->.</span>Generate the first output using the appropriate generative stage.</li><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">5<!-- -->.</span>Review the result for coherence, realism, character identity, environment continuity and narrative alignment.</li><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">6<!-- -->.</span>Refine prompts, assets or direction based on observed weaknesses.</li><li class="flex gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground"><span class="font-bold text-violet-300">7<!-- -->.</span>Finalize only after the output meets the intended standard.</li></ol></div></div></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3"><div class="lg:col-span-1"><div class="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-fuchsia-400/30 bg-fuchsia-500/10 px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-fuchsia-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-clapperboard h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20.2 6 3 11l-.9-2.4c-.3-1.1.3-2.2 1.3-2.5l13.5-4c1.1-.3 2.2.3 2.5 1.3Z"></path><path d="m6.2 5.3 3.1 3.9"></path><path d="m12.4 3.4 3.1 4"></path><path d="M3 11h18v8a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2Z"></path></svg>AI Video Content Creation</div><h3 class="font-display text-xl font-bold text-foreground">AI Video Production: Story, Sequencing and Review</h3><p class="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">AI video production combines story development, scripting, image generation, video generation, voice production, sequencing and review. My work in this area focuses on producing long-form, character-driven content where continuity, realism and narrative alignment matter.</p></div><div class="lg:col-span-2"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-6"><h4 class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">Creative direction</h4><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">AI video workflows require more than writing a prompt and accepting the first result. The process begins with a clear concept: the story, tone, setting, character direction, visual style and intended emotional effect. From there, prompts and assets are refined until the output supports the creative goal — and reviewed critically at every stage before being accepted into the final sequence.</p><h4 class="mb-3 mt-5 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">Quality factors I review</h4><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2"><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Narrative coherence and logical scene progression.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Character recognisability and facial consistency across scenes.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Clothing, styling and visual identity continuity.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Environment, location, background and lighting consistency.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Realism, motion quality and avoidance of obvious AI artifacts.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Voice-to-character fit and audio-visual alignment.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Audience clarity and emotional impact.</span></div><div class="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-2.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg><span class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Alignment between prompt, generated output and final editing direction.</span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="character-consistency" class="relative scroll-mt-24 py-20 sm:py-28 border-t border-border/40"><div class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8"><div class="max-w-3xl"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.18em] bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-200 border-sky-400/30"><span class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-pulse-glow"></span>Character Consistency</div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><h2 class="font-display text-3xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight text-foreground sm:text-4xl md:text-[2.7rem]">Character identity, facial consistency <span class="text-gradient">&amp; scene continuity</span></h2></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><p class="mt-5 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground sm:text-lg">Maintaining character recognisability, facial appearance, clothing, environments, locations and backgrounds across multiple generated scenes is one of the hardest problems in long-form AI content. My approach combines structured prompting, useful references, repeated refinement and critical review at every stage — treating character identity as production discipline, not luck.</p></div></div><div class="mt-12 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12"><div class="lg:col-span-5" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/20 bg-sky-500/5 p-7"><div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-code-xml h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m18 16 4-4-4-4"></path><path d="m6 8-4 4 4 4"></path><path d="m14.5 4-5 16"></path></svg></div><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">My character consistency philosophy</h3></div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Character identity is the foundation of long-form AI storytelling. If a character is not recognisable across scenes, the story breaks — no matter how strong the individual shots are. My approach treats character identity, facial consistency and scene continuity as a discipline: plan the identity before generation, anchor every scene to references, review every output for drift, and correct inconsistencies before they propagate. The goal is to make the audience see the same person in the same world across every scene, even when each scene was generated separately.</p></div></div><div class="lg:col-span-7" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full space-y-4"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-6"><div class="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-violet-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-compass h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m16.24 7.76-1.804 5.411a2 2 0 0 1-1.265 1.265L7.76 16.24l1.804-5.411a2 2 0 0 1 1.265-1.265z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle></svg> Identity methodology</div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">My methodology is built on disciplined visual control: establish the identity first, then structure every scene around it. I begin with the character — facial characteristics, clothing, styling, visual cues — and only then move into environment, location, background and lighting. From there, I generate per scene with a prompt anchored to the established references, review each output critically, and refine until the scene holds together.</p></div><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-6"><div class="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-workflow h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="8" height="8" x="3" y="3" rx="2"></rect><path d="M7 11v4a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h4"></path><rect width="8" height="8" x="13" y="13" rx="2"></rect></svg> Consistency pipeline</div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I treat character consistency as a pipeline, not a single generation. Each scene is one stage in a longer production: identity establishment, reference building, scene generation, drift review, correction and refinement, sequencing, and end-to-end final review. Every stage feeds the next — a weakness at any stage propagates forward, so each stage has its own review checkpoint before the next begins.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/60 p-6 backdrop-blur-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-[0_0_50px_-8px_rgba(59,130,246,0.7)] h-full"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute -right-16 -top-16 h-40 w-40 rounded-full opacity-0 blur-3xl transition-opacity duration-500 group-hover:opacity-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-sky-500 to-indigo-500"></div><div class="relative"><h4 class="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200">Drift analysis</h4><p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">When a character drifts between scenes, I do not guess at the cause. I break the problem into parts: facial characteristics, clothing, styling, lighting, environment, background, motion or artifact. I compare the output against the reference, identify which dimension drifted, trace the cause back to the prompt or reference, and fix that specific issue rather than changing everything blindly.</p></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/60 p-6 backdrop-blur-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-[0_0_50px_-8px_rgba(168,85,247,0.7)] h-full"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute -right-16 -top-16 h-40 w-40 rounded-full opacity-0 blur-3xl transition-opacity duration-500 group-hover:opacity-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-500 to-fuchsia-500"></div><div class="relative"><h4 class="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-violet-200">Audience-first thinking</h4><p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Audience perception is the real test of character consistency. I plan the audience journey before generating any scene: who is this character, what do they look like, what should the audience recognise about them, and where could drift break that recognition? Every scene is then checked against the audience&#x27;s mental model of the character, not just against the prompt.</p></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/60 p-6 backdrop-blur-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-[0_0_50px_-8px_rgba(217,70,239,0.7)] h-full"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute -right-16 -top-16 h-40 w-40 rounded-full opacity-0 blur-3xl transition-opacity duration-500 group-hover:opacity-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-fuchsia-500 to-pink-500"></div><div class="relative"><h4 class="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200">Reference architecture mindset</h4><p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I structure character identity so it can scale. A character established today should still be recognisable in scene 1, scene 20, scene 100 and across future sequels or series. The reference architecture should not lock the production into a single generation run — it should support continuity across a long-form content library.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-violet-200">Identity design philosophy</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Good character consistency is invisible. When it works, the audience does not notice the technical effort — they notice that the same person shows up in every scene, in the same world, with the same identity. My philosophy prioritises recognisability over flashiness: the goal is identity stability, not impressive individual shots that break continuity.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/20 bg-sky-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-zap h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 14a1 1 0 0 1-.78-1.63l9.9-10.2a.5.5 0 0 1 .86.46l-1.92 6.02A1 1 0 0 0 13 10h7a1 1 0 0 1 .78 1.63l-9.9 10.2a.5.5 0 0 1-.86-.46l1.92-6.02A1 1 0 0 0 11 14z"></path></svg> Batch generation strategy</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Batch scene generation works best when identity is anchored first. By establishing the character and references up front, I reduce unnecessary retries later, keep prompt iterations focused, and avoid regenerating whole scenes for avoidable drift.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-fuchsia-400/20 bg-fuchsia-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-shield-check h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1z"></path><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg> Responsible use mindset</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">My character consistency practice is built around responsible use: I do not generate misleading representations of real people, do not replicate identifiable private individuals without authorisation, and keep character use aligned with the intended story, audience and platform.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-emerald-400/20 bg-emerald-500/5 p-6"><h4 class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-200">Consistency quality assurance</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Quality is built into every stage, not added at the end. I review each scene against the brief before accepting it, check the full sequence end-to-end before finalising, and trace any drift back to its cause so the next scene is stronger.</p></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/40 p-7 backdrop-blur-sm"><h3 class="mb-5 font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Character identity lifecycle</h3><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5"><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">1</span>Identity establishment</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Define the character — facial characteristics, clothing, styling, visual cues — before any scene generation begins.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">2</span>Reference building</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Collect useful references for the character, environment, location, background and lighting so each output can be checked against intent.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">3</span>Scene generation</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Produce each scene with a prompt anchored to the established identity and references.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">4</span>Drift review</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Check each output for character recognisability, facial appearance, clothing, environment and background continuity.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">5</span>Correction</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Refine prompts or references and regenerate where drift, distortion or identity loss appears.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">6</span>Sequencing</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Order accepted scenes into a coherent sequence that respects narrative direction and continuity.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">7</span>End-to-end review</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Review the full sequence as one production, checking continuity, pacing, tone and emotional effect end-to-end.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-3"><p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-4 w-4 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[9px] font-bold">8</span>Finalisation</p><p class="mt-1 pl-5.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Accept the production only after it meets the intended quality standard and the character reads as one identity across every scene.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-7"><h3 class="mb-3 font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Character direction consultation</h3><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I begin every character-driven project with a consultation: What does the character look like? Who is the audience? What is the story? What is the intended emotional effect? Based on those answers, I recommend the most practical character consistency approach for the scope and timeline.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-fuchsia-400/20 bg-fuchsia-500/5 p-7"><h3 class="mb-3 font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Character requirement analysis</h3><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Good character requirements lead to consistent characters. I help clients articulate the character&#x27;s identity, visual cues and narrative role. I distinguish must-have identity features from nice-to-have visual details so the production stays focused on recognisability.</p></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/40 p-7 backdrop-blur-sm"><h3 class="mb-5 font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">What I prioritize</h3><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"><div class="rounded-xl border border-violet-500/30 bg-violet-500/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-violet-300">Character recognisability</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">The audience should see the same person across every scene.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-500/30 bg-fuchsia-500/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-300">Facial consistency</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Facial characteristics should not drift, morph or break identity between scenes.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-sky-300">Clothing continuity</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Wardrobe, accessories and styling should remain coherent with the story.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-slate-400/30 bg-slate-400/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200">Environment continuity</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Locations, backgrounds, settings and objects should remain consistent across scenes.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-violet-500/30 bg-violet-500/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-violet-300">Narrative alignment</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Each scene should serve the story, tone and intended emotional effect.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-500/30 bg-fuchsia-500/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-300">Critical review</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Every output is reviewed against the brief — not accepted on first impression.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-sky-300">Iterative refinement</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Prompts and references are revised based on observed drift, not guesswork.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-slate-400/30 bg-slate-400/10 p-4"><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200">End-to-end continuity</p><p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">The full sequence is reviewed as one production, not as isolated shots.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-5"><h4 class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-violet-200">Reference maintainability</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">A character identity becomes more valuable when it can be reused across scenes, sequels and future productions. I structure references, prompts and review notes so the identity remains stable and recoverable over time, not just for a single generation run.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-5"><h4 class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200">Cross-scene scalability</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I build character identity for scale. The reference architecture should support the same character across many scenes, long-form productions, sequels and series — without requiring a complete redesign each time.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="h-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-5"><h4 class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200">Long-term identity support</h4><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">A character identity is not a one-time deliverable — it is a living reference. I plan for long-term support: updated references, prompt refinements, review notes and continuity documentation so the character stays recognisable across future productions.</p></div></div></div><div class="mt-10"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><h3 class="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-foreground sm:text-2xl">Character consistency solutions for every production</h3><p class="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I do not force every production into the same character consistency approach. I evaluate the story, scope, timeline and future growth path, then recommend the most practical reference and review architecture for that specific production.</p></div><div class="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/60 p-6 border border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-sky-300">Continuity documentation</span></div></div></div><p class="mt-5 border-t border-border/50 pt-4 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Character consistency scales with planning. A small production can stay lightweight — a few references and a clear identity brief. Larger productions benefit from a fuller reference architecture: detailed character sheets, environment references, review checkpoints and continuity documentation. Advanced techniques should be added only when the production scope, timeline and future growth justify them.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/40 p-7 backdrop-blur-sm"><div class="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-sparkles h-5 w-5 text-fuchsia-300" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9.937 15.5A2 2 0 0 0 8.5 14.063l-6.135-1.582a.5.5 0 0 1 0-.962L8.5 9.936A2 2 0 0 0 9.937 8.5l1.582-6.135a.5.5 0 0 1 .963 0L14.063 8.5A2 2 0 0 0 15.5 9.937l6.135 1.581a.5.5 0 0 1 0 .964L15.5 14.063a2 2 0 0 0-1.437 1.437l-1.582 6.135a.5.5 0 0 1-.963 0z"></path><path d="M20 3v4"></path><path d="M22 5h-4"></path><path d="M4 17v2"></path><path d="M5 18H3"></path></svg><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Advanced Character Consistency Techniques</h3></div><p class="mb-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Depending on the production&#x27;s scope, timeline and long-term goal, these character consistency techniques can be applied gradually. visual cues consistent across the long-form production.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-950/40 to-fuchsia-950/30 p-7"><h4 class="mb-2 font-display text-base font-semibold text-foreground">Solution recommendation</h4><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I do not force every production into the same character consistency approach. I evaluate the story, scope, timeline and future growth path, then recommend the most practical reference and review architecture for that specific production.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-950/40 to-fuchsia-950/30 p-7 text-center sm:flex-row sm:text-left"><div><h4 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Need a character-driven AI production built around your story?</h4><p class="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Let&#x27;s discuss your characters, scenes, audience and continuity goals.</p></div><button class="group inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-full Discussion<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" 7"></path></svg></button></div></div></div></section><section id="quality-control" class="relative scroll-mt-24 py-20 sm:py-28 border-t border-border/40"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden"><div class="absolute left-1/2 top-0 h-72 w-72 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-violet-700/10 blur-[120px]"></div></div><div class="relative mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8"><div class="max-w-3xl"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.18em] bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-200 border-violet-400/30"><span class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-pulse-glow"></span>Quality Control &amp; Content Review</div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><h2 class="font-display text-3xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight text-foreground sm:text-4xl md:text-[2.7rem]">Guided by evidence, critical review<!-- --> <span class="text-gradient">&amp; clear quality communication</span></h2></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><p class="mt-5 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground sm:text-lg">Quality control for generative AI combines content research, critical review, model stumping and prompt stress testing. My approach focuses on identifying weaknesses, inconsistencies and failures in AI outputs — and correcting them before they reach a final production or audience.</p></div></div><div class="mt-12 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12"><div class="space-y-6 lg:col-span-5"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-violet-400/20 bg-violet-500/5 p-7"><div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-violet-400/30 bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-shield-check h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1z"></path><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg></div><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Quality control philosophy</h3></div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Quality control is not a final checkbox. It is a discipline that runs through every stage of a generative AI production. I plan before generation, keep useful references, review outputs critically, identify where a model fails under demanding prompts, and correct inconsistencies before moving forward. My background in content writing, research and digital content quality review (Upwork, TELUS Digital, Remotasks) shapes how I approach this work.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-7"><div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-400/30 bg-fuchsia-500/15 text-fuchsia-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-file-warning h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 2H6a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v16a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V7Z"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg></div><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Reporting style</h3></div><p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">My reporting style focuses on clarity and usefulness: what the weakness is, where it appears, why it matters, what evidence supports the finding, how it can be reproduced, and what correction or refinement is recommended. The goal is always to make the next output stronger, not just to flag the current one as weak.</p></div></div><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-amber-400/20 bg-amber-500/5 p-6"><div class="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-eye h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2.062 12.348a1 1 0 0 1 0-.696 10.75 10.75 0 0 1 19.876 0 1 1 0 0 1 0 .696 10.75 10.75 0 0 1-19.876 0"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"></circle></svg>What I can discuss publicly</div><p class="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">I can discuss my general methodology, quality-control principles, model stumping approach, prompt stress testing, and review process. I do not expose confidential client content, private productions, internal review data or unpublished assets unless explicitly authorized.</p></div></div></div><div class="space-y-6 lg:col-span-7"><div style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px)"><div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card/50 p-7"><div class="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lock h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="18" height="11" x="3" y="11" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v4"></path></svg></div><h3 class="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-foreground">Methodology overview</h3></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2"><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">1</span>Define the standard</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Clarify what a successful output looks like — character identity, environment, narrative alignment, realism, audience suitability — before any review.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">2</span>Review critically</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Inspect each output against the standard: character recognisability, facial appearance, clothing, environment, background, motion, narrative alignment, artifact risk.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">3</span>Identify weaknesses</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Look for drift, distortion, identity loss, environment breaks, unsupported claims, weak reasoning, ambiguity, overconfidence or inconsistency.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">4</span>Stress-test prompts</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Probe the model with difficult, ambiguous or demanding instructions to surface where it fails, hallucinates or produces inconsistent behaviour.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">5</span>Trace the cause</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Identify whether the weakness came from the prompt, the reference, the tool limitation or the editing process.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">6</span>Correct and refine</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Revise the prompt, references or direction to address the specific weakness rather than changing everything blindly.</p></div><div class="rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-4"><p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-sky-200"><span class="grid h-5 w-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border border-sky-400/40 bg-background text-[10px] font-bold">7</span>Final review</p><p class="mt-1 pl-7 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">Review the corrected output against the standard and the longer sequence it belongs
